955 entries, add-state-park-loader to add-unknown-phrase. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **add-state-park-loader**: Use this skill when adding a new state-park photo loader to bring a US state's official park gallery into beach_photos. Triggers include "write a state-park loader for &lt;STATE&gt;", "add NH state parks photos", "build a state parks loader", or any work involving scripts/loaders/&lt;state&gt;.py + scripts/state_park_urls.json. DO NOT use for non-state-park photo sources (Flickr / Wikimedia / Tavily — those are state-agnostic loaders that already exist), for dog-park photos (dp_photos_* ops in dog-park-state-launch), or for non-photo state-park enrichment (codify-state covers state-agency dog policy). ([beachfranz/dog-beach-claude](https://github.com/beachfranz/dog-beach-claude/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/add-state-park-loader) · ★1)
